<script>
function notification_toggle(){
  $('.notification_div').toggleClass('hide_notification');
  $('.allRead').slideToggle();
  $('.notification_menu').toggleClass('arrow');
  $('.notification_desktop').toggleClass('arrow');
  $('.width_hover').toggleClass('large');
}
$('.notification_menu').on("click", function(){
  notification_toggle();
})
$('.dropbtn').on("click", function(){
  $('#myNotification').toggleClass('display-me');
  $('.notification_div').addClass('hide_notification');
  $('.allRead').slideToggle();
  $('.notification_menu').addClass('arrow');
  $('.notification_desktop').removeClass('arrow');
  $('.width_hover').removeClass('large');
})

<script>
  $("#header_search_btn").click(function(e){
    e.preventDefault();
    if ($("#txtSearch").val() == ""){
      $("#txtSearch").focus();
      return
    }
    url = window.location.origin + '/doctors';
    url += "/" + city.toLowerCase();
    if (area != "")
    {
      url += "/" + area.toLowerCase();
    }
    url += "/" + $("#txtSearch").val().toLowerCase();
    url = url.replace(/ /g, "-");
    window.location.href = url
  })
  

  $(document).ready(function(){
    $('.menu-bar').on('click', function(e){
      e.stopPropagation();
      $('.header-leftnav').animate({"left": "0px"});
    });
    $('.header-leftnav-close').on('click', function(e){
      $('.header-leftnav').animate({"left": "-400px"});
    });
    $(document).click(function(){
      $('.header-leftnav').animate({"left": "-400px"});
    });
    
    $('.navbar-nav-custom .dropdown').hover(function() {
      $(this).find('.dropdown-menu').first().stop(true, true).delay(250).slideDown();
    }, function() {
      $(this).find('.dropdown-menu').first().stop(true, true).delay(100).slideUp()
    });

//search code

  $('.pinsearch--bg,.allsearch--bg').on('focus',function(){
  $('body').prepend('<div class="search-overlay"></div>');
  });

  $('.pinsearch--bg,.allsearch--bg').on('focusout', function(e){
      $('.search-overlay').remove();
  });
  

  });
</script>

<script>
  function chatbot_Speak(value, user_input){
    App.room.speak({"path_id": value}, user_input)
    PlaySound();
    $('#chat_landing').hide();
    $('#chat_internal').show();
    var clicked_item = localStorage.setItem("clicked_item", user_input);
  }
  function PlaySound() {
    var sound = document.getElementById("audio");
    sound.play()
  }
</script>

<script>
  var BOT_BUTTON_CLICKED = false;
  $(document).ready(function(){
    setTimeout(function() {//calls click event after a certain time
      if (BOT_BUTTON_CLICKED == false) {
        $('.bot_popup').toggle(1500);
    }
    }, 5000);
    if (localStorage.getItem("chatbot-opened") != "true"){
      //chatbot opened in 5 seconds
      setTimeout(function(){
        $('.bot_popup').click();
        localStorage.setItem("chatbot-opened","true");
      }, 5000)
    } 
  });
  $(document).ready(function(){
    $("#chat_bot_icon").show();
  });
  
  $("#chatbot_resize_button").on('click', function(){
    var chatbot_width = 400;
    $("#chat_bot").width() == 400 ? chatbot_width = 600 : chatbot_width = 400;
    $("#chat_bot").addClass('expand').width(chatbot_width);
    // $('.bottom_btns').css('max-width',chatbot_width);
  });
  $("#chat_bot_icon").click( function(){
    $("#chat_bot").toggle();
    wtf = $('#scroll_message');
    height = wtf[0].scrollHeight;
    wtf.scrollTop(height)
    // hide pop-up message once button has been clicked
    $('.bot_popup').hide();
    BOT_BUTTON_CLICKED = true;
  });

  $('.bot_popup').click( function(e){
    if($(e.target).is("span.pop-up-close")) {
      $('.bot_popup').hide();
      return true;
    }
    $("#chat_bot").toggle();
    $('.bot_popup').hide();
    BOT_BUTTON_CLICKED = true;
    wtf = $('#scroll_message');
    height = wtf[0].scrollHeight;
    wtf.scrollTop(height)
  });
</script>
<script>  
  function reload_display(){
    $.ajax(
        {
          url: "/reload_chat.js",
          type: 'GET',
          data: {chat_Session_id: '' }
        });
  }
  $("#talktous").on('click', function(){
     App.room.speak({"path_id": 47}, "Talk to medical expert");
     PlaySound();
  });
  $('.back_btn').hover(function(){
    $(".back_btn").attr('title', 'Restart');
  });
  $('.chat_service').on('click', function(){
    $('.talk_to_us').show();
    /* hide talk to medical expert */
    //$('.talk_to_us').hide();
    // if(localStorage.getItem('clicked_item') == "Talk to a Medical Expert"){
    //   $('.talk_to_us').hide();
    // }
    // else{
    //   $('.talk_to_us').show();
    // } 
  })
  function PlaySound() {
    var sound = document.getElementById("audio");
    sound.play()
  }
  $(document).ready(function(){
    //localStorage.removeItem('clicked_item');
    $('.talk_to_us').hide();
    /* highlighter */
    setIdleTimeout(10000, function() {
        $("[class^='highlight_remind_']").show();
      }, function() {
        $("[class^='highlight_remind_']").hide();
    });

    function setIdleTimeout(millis, onIdle, onUnidle) {
      var timeout = 0;
      startTimer();

      function startTimer() {
        timeout = setTimeout(onExpires, millis);
        document.addEventListener("mousemove", onActivity);
        document.addEventListener("keypress", onActivity);
      }

      function onExpires() {
        timeout = 0;
        onIdle();
      }

      function onActivity() {
        if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
        else onUnidle();
        document.removeEventListener("mousemove", onActivity);
        document.removeEventListener("keypress", onActivity);
        document.removeEventListener("scroll", onActivity);
        document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onActivity);
        document.removeEventListener("touchstart", onActivity);
        setTimeout(startTimer, 1000);
      }
    }
  })
</script>
